Команда работала слаженно.<br><small style="color:#888">RU→EN</small>	<b>The team worked seamlessly.</b><hr><small>⚠ частая ошибка: <i>The team worked in a well-coordinated manner.</i><br>'слаженно' is best rendered as 'seamlessly' or 'in sync'; 'in a well-coordinated manner' is overly formal and bureaucratic for describing teamwork.</small>	translation RU_EN wrong_register
